        Idea:
Two changes to the video editor's shot grouping behavior.

**Change 1: Restrict per-clip right-click menu when clip is inside a pinned shot group**

Background: A "pinned shot group" visually groups clips on the timeline under a shot label. The shot label has its own context menu (ShotGroupContextMenu) for shot-level operations (Deconstruct, Delete, Switch to Final Video, Generate Video, etc.). The per-clip right-click menu (ClipContextMenu in src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx) currently shows shot-management actions (Create Shot, list of shots, Generate Video, Jump to Shot) AND timeline-geometry actions (Split Here, Delete Clip) AND asset-state actions (Update to current variant, Dismiss reminder) regardless of whether the clip is in a pinned shot group.

Principle: A pinned shot group is the unit of timeline editing. Clips inside it are no longer first-class timeline citizens; they're 'images inside this shot.' So per-clip operations affecting timeline geometry or shot membership don't belong on the clip's right-click menu when it's in a shot group.

Required behavior when isInPinnedShotGroup === true:
- Show ONLY asset/variant state actions: 'Update to current variant' and 'Dismiss reminder' (when applicable).
- HIDE: Split Here, Delete Clip, Delete N clips, Mute/unmute, Create Shot, list of existing shots with Jump/Generate buttons, Generate Video.
- If neither asset-state action applies, the right-click should be a no-op (don't open an empty menu).

When isInPinnedShotGroup === false -> current behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- shotGroupClipIds: Set<string> already exists in TimelineEditor.tsx (around line 321).
- getActionRender in TimelineEditor.tsx (~line 542) renders each <ClipAction>. Pass new prop isInPinnedShotGroup={shotGroupClipIds.has(action.id)}.
- Add isInPinnedShotGroup?: boolean to ClipAction props and thread it into ClipContextMenuProps.
- In ClipContextMenu render (ClipAction.tsx:74-206), gate the hidden sections on !isInPinnedShotGroup.
- In ClipActionComponent.handleContextMenu, if isInPinnedShotGroup AND no asset-state actions, early-return without opening menu.
- Tests in ClipAction.test.tsx may need updates.

**Change 2: Auto-shift adjacent clips inside a shot group to keep the group contiguous when one is resized**

Background: A pinned shot group requires constituent clips to be contiguous. Validation in TimelineCanvas.tsx positionedShotGroups (around line 425-477) drops a group if any sibling pair has a gap. When user resizes a middle clip's edge inward, it creates a gap and group disappears.

Required behavior: When user resizes an edge of a clip inside a pinned shot group:
- If drag LEFT edge of middle/last clip -> previous clip's RIGHT edge follows, eating the gap.
- If drag RIGHT edge of middle/first clip -> next clip's LEFT edge follows.
- For FIRST clip in group, its LEFT edge is free. For LAST clip, its RIGHT edge is free.
- If clip NOT in pinned shot group, behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- Resize state in TimelineCanvas.tsx: handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, resizeSessionRef, resizeOverrides, ~lines 549-720.
- During updateResize, compute override for adjacent clip in shot group.
- Enforce min-duration on adjacent clip.
- Call setResizeOverrides for BOTH clips.
- On endResize, both overrides flushed via onActionResizeEnd. May need to extend onActionResizeEnd to take array, or call twice.
- Find shot group via shotGroups prop.
- Skip snap-to-sibling-within-group (auto-shift replaces it).

Files likely to change:
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.test.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.test.tsx

Constraints:
- Scoped to video editor tool only.
- No DB/migration/edge function changes.
- Follow CLAUDE.md rules.
- Do not regress existing tests (one pre-existing failure about data-shot-group-kind='pinned' is unrelated, leave it).

- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/lib/resolve-overlaps.ts:139-195` at the `useclipresize` call site. this api only produces `metapatches[clipid]` for the moved clip, so revised step 3's generic 'for each clip id in overlappatches' loop is broader than the actual resize surface and adds complexity the fix does not currently need. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the existing video-drop paths in `src/tools/video-editor/lib/external-drop-utils.ts:46-55,386-412` and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:198-252`. the broader repository pattern for video assets is not just “register then insert”; multi-generation video drops also patch `registry.duration` using `getdroppedgenerationduration()`, while `useshotfinalvideos()` in `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37` currently fetches no duration metadata. that means final-video replacement is broader than badge wiring alone if the new clip is expected to behave like other video assets in timing ui and resize logic. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked related asset-insertion code in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:150-271` and `src/tools/video-editor/lib/coordinate-utils.ts:24-33`. the broader repository pattern for adding timeline media is centralized there, including track compatibility, manual-track defaults, selection updates, and `cliporder` maintenance, so the revised plan is still broader than a pure menu/ui change and needs either shared helper extraction or a more explicit statement that those invariants are being replicated on purpose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: cross-track group motion is broader than step 18 currently states. because the pinned-group model now includes `trackid`, every move path that can relocate a grouped clip set across rows has to update pinned-group metadata too; otherwise the overlay and menu logic will still reference the previous track even though the clip actions moved successfully. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: if phases 6 and 7 are deferred, the shipped work becomes a narrower "pin existing inferred groups and toggle them" feature rather than the full brief, which also called out shot-drop creation and shot-image auto-sync. that broader scope tension is still present in the revised plan. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the movement surface beyond `useclipdrag`. the broader problem is not just drag-threshold expansion; both grouped multi-drag and single-clip cross-track move helpers can relocate clips across rows, so every move path that changes a pinned group's row membership has to update pinned-group metadata too, and the revised plan still scopes that too narrowly. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] shot-group-badge-scope: shot-group badge scope: step 3 suppresses the reminder at shot level, but this repo can render multiple shot groups for the same shot, so switching one group to the final video would hide the badge on other groups of that shot too. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: task reference matching: reusing `taskreferencesgeneration` as the only matcher will miss active video-generation tasks for source clips. the helper does not inspect `input_image_generation_ids`, `start_image_generation_id`, or `end_image_generation_id`, even though this repo stores and later reads those fields to identify which timeline generations a travel task is using. that means spinners would fail to appear for at least some travel/video tasks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-media-duration: timeline media duration: the revised plan still sizes the final-video clip to the shot-group span without any duration source, so shorter final videos can still overrun their real media length. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-shot-group-cleanup: timeline shot-group cleanup: the plan only updates `useshotgroups.test.ts`, but the repository also has direct test coverage for the removed `resolvefinalvideoshotids()` helper and the `timelinecanvas` suggested-group/finalvideoshotids api. without explicitly updating `timelineeditor.test.ts` and `timelinecanvas.test.tsx`, the plan leaves the supporting test surface behind and the post-change suite will fail on deleted symbols and obsolete expectations. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-signatures: timeline signatures: applying `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` after `materializedata()` would leave `stablesignature` and other config-derived state stale unless the code rebuilds `timelinedata` from the updated config. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-video-duration: timeline-video-duration: the plan routes dropped videos through a new generation path but does not preserve duration metadata in the asset registry. in this repo, inserted video clips fall back to 5 seconds when `registry.duration` is missing, and the existing multi-generation video path explicitly patches duration from drag metadata before dropping. following the plan as written would still create generation-backed videos that are timed incorrectly on the timeline. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Two changes to the video editor's shot grouping behavior.

**Change 1: Restrict per-clip right-click menu when clip is inside a pinned shot group**

Background: A "pinned shot group" visually groups clips on the timeline under a shot label. The shot label has its own context menu (ShotGroupContextMenu) for shot-level operations (Deconstruct, Delete, Switch to Final Video, Generate Video, etc.). The per-clip right-click menu (ClipContextMenu in src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx) currently shows shot-management actions (Create Shot, list of shots, Generate Video, Jump to Shot) AND timeline-geometry actions (Split Here, Delete Clip) AND asset-state actions (Update to current variant, Dismiss reminder) regardless of whether the clip is in a pinned shot group.

Principle: A pinned shot group is the unit of timeline editing. Clips inside it are no longer first-class timeline citizens; they're 'images inside this shot.' So per-clip operations affecting timeline geometry or shot membership don't belong on the clip's right-click menu when it's in a shot group.

Required behavior when isInPinnedShotGroup === true:
- Show ONLY asset/variant state actions: 'Update to current variant' and 'Dismiss reminder' (when applicable).
- HIDE: Split Here, Delete Clip, Delete N clips, Mute/unmute, Create Shot, list of existing shots with Jump/Generate buttons, Generate Video.
- If neither asset-state action applies, the right-click should be a no-op (don't open an empty menu).

When isInPinnedShotGroup === false -> current behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- shotGroupClipIds: Set<string> already exists in TimelineEditor.tsx (around line 321).
- getActionRender in TimelineEditor.tsx (~line 542) renders each <ClipAction>. Pass new prop isInPinnedShotGroup={shotGroupClipIds.has(action.id)}.
- Add isInPinnedShotGroup?: boolean to ClipAction props and thread it into ClipContextMenuProps.
- In ClipContextMenu render (ClipAction.tsx:74-206), gate the hidden sections on !isInPinnedShotGroup.
- In ClipActionComponent.handleContextMenu, if isInPinnedShotGroup AND no asset-state actions, early-return without opening menu.
- Tests in ClipAction.test.tsx may need updates.

**Change 2: Auto-shift adjacent clips inside a shot group to keep the group contiguous when one is resized**

Background: A pinned shot group requires constituent clips to be contiguous. Validation in TimelineCanvas.tsx positionedShotGroups (around line 425-477) drops a group if any sibling pair has a gap. When user resizes a middle clip's edge inward, it creates a gap and group disappears.

Required behavior: When user resizes an edge of a clip inside a pinned shot group:
- If drag LEFT edge of middle/last clip -> previous clip's RIGHT edge follows, eating the gap.
- If drag RIGHT edge of middle/first clip -> next clip's LEFT edge follows.
- For FIRST clip in group, its LEFT edge is free. For LAST clip, its RIGHT edge is free.
- If clip NOT in pinned shot group, behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- Resize state in TimelineCanvas.tsx: handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, resizeSessionRef, resizeOverrides, ~lines 549-720.
- During updateResize, compute override for adjacent clip in shot group.
- Enforce min-duration on adjacent clip.
- Call setResizeOverrides for BOTH clips.
- On endResize, both overrides flushed via onActionResizeEnd. May need to extend onActionResizeEnd to take array, or call twice.
- Find shot group via shotGroups prop.
- Skip snap-to-sibling-within-group (auto-shift replaces it).

Files likely to change:
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.test.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.test.tsx

Constraints:
- Scoped to video editor tool only.
- No DB/migration/edge function changes.
- Follow CLAUDE.md rules.
- Do not regress existing tests (one pre-existing failure about data-shot-group-kind='pinned' is unrelated, leave it).

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Two changes to the video editor's shot grouping behavior.

**Change 1: Restrict per-clip right-click menu when clip is inside a pinned shot group**

Background: A "pinned shot group" visually groups clips on the timeline under a shot label. The shot label has its own context menu (ShotGroupContextMenu) for shot-level operations (Deconstruct, Delete, Switch to Final Video, Generate Video, etc.). The per-clip right-click menu (ClipContextMenu in src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx) currently shows shot-management actions (Create Shot, list of shots, Generate Video, Jump to Shot) AND timeline-geometry actions (Split Here, Delete Clip) AND asset-state actions (Update to current variant, Dismiss reminder) regardless of whether the clip is in a pinned shot group.

Principle: A pinned shot group is the unit of timeline editing. Clips inside it are no longer first-class timeline citizens; they're 'images inside this shot.' So per-clip operations affecting timeline geometry or shot membership don't belong on the clip's right-click menu when it's in a shot group.

Required behavior when isInPinnedShotGroup === true:
- Show ONLY asset/variant state actions: 'Update to current variant' and 'Dismiss reminder' (when applicable).
- HIDE: Split Here, Delete Clip, Delete N clips, Mute/unmute, Create Shot, list of existing shots with Jump/Generate buttons, Generate Video.
- If neither asset-state action applies, the right-click should be a no-op (don't open an empty menu).

When isInPinnedShotGroup === false -> current behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- shotGroupClipIds: Set<string> already exists in TimelineEditor.tsx (around line 321).
- getActionRender in TimelineEditor.tsx (~line 542) renders each <ClipAction>. Pass new prop isInPinnedShotGroup={shotGroupClipIds.has(action.id)}.
- Add isInPinnedShotGroup?: boolean to ClipAction props and thread it into ClipContextMenuProps.
- In ClipContextMenu render (ClipAction.tsx:74-206), gate the hidden sections on !isInPinnedShotGroup.
- In ClipActionComponent.handleContextMenu, if isInPinnedShotGroup AND no asset-state actions, early-return without opening menu.
- Tests in ClipAction.test.tsx may need updates.

**Change 2: Auto-shift adjacent clips inside a shot group to keep the group contiguous when one is resized**

Background: A pinned shot group requires constituent clips to be contiguous. Validation in TimelineCanvas.tsx positionedShotGroups (around line 425-477) drops a group if any sibling pair has a gap. When user resizes a middle clip's edge inward, it creates a gap and group disappears.

Required behavior: When user resizes an edge of a clip inside a pinned shot group:
- If drag LEFT edge of middle/last clip -> previous clip's RIGHT edge follows, eating the gap.
- If drag RIGHT edge of middle/first clip -> next clip's LEFT edge follows.
- For FIRST clip in group, its LEFT edge is free. For LAST clip, its RIGHT edge is free.
- If clip NOT in pinned shot group, behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- Resize state in TimelineCanvas.tsx: handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, resizeSessionRef, resizeOverrides, ~lines 549-720.
- During updateResize, compute override for adjacent clip in shot group.
- Enforce min-duration on adjacent clip.
- Call setResizeOverrides for BOTH clips.
- On endResize, both overrides flushed via onActionResizeEnd. May need to extend onActionResizeEnd to take array, or call twice.
- Find shot group via shotGroups prop.
- Skip snap-to-sibling-within-group (auto-shift replaces it).

Files likely to change:
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.test.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.test.tsx

Constraints:
- Scoped to video editor tool only.
- No DB/migration/edge function changes.
- Follow CLAUDE.md rules.
- Do not regress existing tests (one pre-existing failure about data-shot-group-kind='pinned' is unrelated, leave it).

        Idea:
Two changes to the video editor's shot grouping behavior.

**Change 1: Restrict per-clip right-click menu when clip is inside a pinned shot group**

Background: A "pinned shot group" visually groups clips on the timeline under a shot label. The shot label has its own context menu (ShotGroupContextMenu) for shot-level operations (Deconstruct, Delete, Switch to Final Video, Generate Video, etc.). The per-clip right-click menu (ClipContextMenu in src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx) currently shows shot-management actions (Create Shot, list of shots, Generate Video, Jump to Shot) AND timeline-geometry actions (Split Here, Delete Clip) AND asset-state actions (Update to current variant, Dismiss reminder) regardless of whether the clip is in a pinned shot group.

Principle: A pinned shot group is the unit of timeline editing. Clips inside it are no longer first-class timeline citizens; they're 'images inside this shot.' So per-clip operations affecting timeline geometry or shot membership don't belong on the clip's right-click menu when it's in a shot group.

Required behavior when isInPinnedShotGroup === true:
- Show ONLY asset/variant state actions: 'Update to current variant' and 'Dismiss reminder' (when applicable).
- HIDE: Split Here, Delete Clip, Delete N clips, Mute/unmute, Create Shot, list of existing shots with Jump/Generate buttons, Generate Video.
- If neither asset-state action applies, the right-click should be a no-op (don't open an empty menu).

When isInPinnedShotGroup === false -> current behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- shotGroupClipIds: Set<string> already exists in TimelineEditor.tsx (around line 321).
- getActionRender in TimelineEditor.tsx (~line 542) renders each <ClipAction>. Pass new prop isInPinnedShotGroup={shotGroupClipIds.has(action.id)}.
- Add isInPinnedShotGroup?: boolean to ClipAction props and thread it into ClipContextMenuProps.
- In ClipContextMenu render (ClipAction.tsx:74-206), gate the hidden sections on !isInPinnedShotGroup.
- In ClipActionComponent.handleContextMenu, if isInPinnedShotGroup AND no asset-state actions, early-return without opening menu.
- Tests in ClipAction.test.tsx may need updates.

**Change 2: Auto-shift adjacent clips inside a shot group to keep the group contiguous when one is resized**

Background: A pinned shot group requires constituent clips to be contiguous. Validation in TimelineCanvas.tsx positionedShotGroups (around line 425-477) drops a group if any sibling pair has a gap. When user resizes a middle clip's edge inward, it creates a gap and group disappears.

Required behavior: When user resizes an edge of a clip inside a pinned shot group:
- If drag LEFT edge of middle/last clip -> previous clip's RIGHT edge follows, eating the gap.
- If drag RIGHT edge of middle/first clip -> next clip's LEFT edge follows.
- For FIRST clip in group, its LEFT edge is free. For LAST clip, its RIGHT edge is free.
- If clip NOT in pinned shot group, behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- Resize state in TimelineCanvas.tsx: handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, resizeSessionRef, resizeOverrides, ~lines 549-720.
- During updateResize, compute override for adjacent clip in shot group.
- Enforce min-duration on adjacent clip.
- Call setResizeOverrides for BOTH clips.
- On endResize, both overrides flushed via onActionResizeEnd. May need to extend onActionResizeEnd to take array, or call twice.
- Find shot group via shotGroups prop.
- Skip snap-to-sibling-within-group (auto-shift replaces it).

Files likely to change:
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.test.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.test.tsx

Constraints:
- Scoped to video editor tool only.
- No DB/migration/edge function changes.
- Follow CLAUDE.md rules.
- Do not regress existing tests (one pre-existing failure about data-shot-group-kind='pinned' is unrelated, leave it).

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
Two changes to the video editor's shot grouping behavior.

**Change 1: Restrict per-clip right-click menu when clip is inside a pinned shot group**

Background: A "pinned shot group" visually groups clips on the timeline under a shot label. The shot label has its own context menu (ShotGroupContextMenu) for shot-level operations (Deconstruct, Delete, Switch to Final Video, Generate Video, etc.). The per-clip right-click menu (ClipContextMenu in src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx) currently shows shot-management actions (Create Shot, list of shots, Generate Video, Jump to Shot) AND timeline-geometry actions (Split Here, Delete Clip) AND asset-state actions (Update to current variant, Dismiss reminder) regardless of whether the clip is in a pinned shot group.

Principle: A pinned shot group is the unit of timeline editing. Clips inside it are no longer first-class timeline citizens; they're 'images inside this shot.' So per-clip operations affecting timeline geometry or shot membership don't belong on the clip's right-click menu when it's in a shot group.

Required behavior when isInPinnedShotGroup === true:
- Show ONLY asset/variant state actions: 'Update to current variant' and 'Dismiss reminder' (when applicable).
- HIDE: Split Here, Delete Clip, Delete N clips, Mute/unmute, Create Shot, list of existing shots with Jump/Generate buttons, Generate Video.
- If neither asset-state action applies, the right-click should be a no-op (don't open an empty menu).

When isInPinnedShotGroup === false -> current behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- shotGroupClipIds: Set<string> already exists in TimelineEditor.tsx (around line 321).
- getActionRender in TimelineEditor.tsx (~line 542) renders each <ClipAction>. Pass new prop isInPinnedShotGroup={shotGroupClipIds.has(action.id)}.
- Add isInPinnedShotGroup?: boolean to ClipAction props and thread it into ClipContextMenuProps.
- In ClipContextMenu render (ClipAction.tsx:74-206), gate the hidden sections on !isInPinnedShotGroup.
- In ClipActionComponent.handleContextMenu, if isInPinnedShotGroup AND no asset-state actions, early-return without opening menu.
- Tests in ClipAction.test.tsx may need updates.

**Change 2: Auto-shift adjacent clips inside a shot group to keep the group contiguous when one is resized**

Background: A pinned shot group requires constituent clips to be contiguous. Validation in TimelineCanvas.tsx positionedShotGroups (around line 425-477) drops a group if any sibling pair has a gap. When user resizes a middle clip's edge inward, it creates a gap and group disappears.

Required behavior: When user resizes an edge of a clip inside a pinned shot group:
- If drag LEFT edge of middle/last clip -> previous clip's RIGHT edge follows, eating the gap.
- If drag RIGHT edge of middle/first clip -> next clip's LEFT edge follows.
- For FIRST clip in group, its LEFT edge is free. For LAST clip, its RIGHT edge is free.
- If clip NOT in pinned shot group, behavior unchanged.

Implementation notes:
- Resize state in TimelineCanvas.tsx: handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, resizeSessionRef, resizeOverrides, ~lines 549-720.
- During updateResize, compute override for adjacent clip in shot group.
- Enforce min-duration on adjacent clip.
- Call setResizeOverrides for BOTH clips.
- On endResize, both overrides flushed via onActionResizeEnd. May need to extend onActionResizeEnd to take array, or call twice.
- Find shot group via shotGroups prop.
- Skip snap-to-sibling-within-group (auto-shift replaces it).

Files likely to change:
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.test.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.test.tsx

Constraints:
- Scoped to video editor tool only.
- No DB/migration/edge function changes.
- Follow CLAUDE.md rules.
- Do not regress existing tests (one pre-existing failure about data-shot-group-kind='pinned' is unrelated, leave it).
